+package com.example.ahakey.model;
+
+import javafx.beans.property.*;
+import javafx.collections.FXCollections;
+import javafx.collections.ObservableMap;
+import java.util.Arrays;
+
+/** Mutated only on the FX thread; worker results carry a connection/load generation. */
+public final class DisplayConfigState {
+    public final ObservableMap<Integer, OledResourceState> resources = FXCollections.observableHashMap();
+    public final IntegerProperty group = new SimpleIntegerProperty(0);
+    public final IntegerProperty substate = new SimpleIntegerProperty(0);
+    public final IntegerProperty selectedEntry = new SimpleIntegerProperty(0);
+    public final IntegerProperty revision = new SimpleIntegerProperty();
+    public final BooleanProperty busy = new SimpleBooleanProperty();
+    public final BooleanProperty loading = new SimpleBooleanProperty();
+    public final BooleanProperty ready = new SimpleBooleanProperty();
+    public final BooleanProperty writeReady = new SimpleBooleanProperty();
+    public final BooleanProperty capabilityReady = new SimpleBooleanProperty();
+    public final StringProperty disabledReasonKey = new SimpleStringProperty("display.identity-disconnected");
+    public final StringProperty message = new SimpleStringProperty("");
+    public final StringProperty progress = new SimpleStringProperty("");
+    public final IntegerProperty brightness = new SimpleIntegerProperty(35);
+    public final IntegerProperty simulatedEffect = new SimpleIntegerProperty(-1);
+    public final IntegerProperty simulatedBrightness = new SimpleIntegerProperty(35);
+    public final LongProperty previewUntilNanos = new SimpleLongProperty();
+    public final BooleanProperty devicePreview = new SimpleBooleanProperty();
+    private boolean brightnessDirty;
+    private long brightnessRevision;
+    private final long[][] effectRevisions = new long[4][9];
+    private boolean previewSimulation;
+    private final int[][] effects = new int[4][9];
+    private final boolean[][] effectDirty = new boolean[4][9];
+
+    public DisplayConfigState() {
+        for (int i = 0; i < 19; i++) resources.put(i, OledResourceState.empty(i));
+        for (int[] mode : effects) Arrays.fill(mode, -1);
+    }
+    public void select(int mode, int newGroup, int state) {
+        if (busy.get()) return;
+        if (mode < 0 || mode > 3 || newGroup < 0 || newGroup > 2 || state < 0 || state > 2)
+            throw new IllegalArgumentException("OLED selection");
+        group.set(newGroup);
+        substate.set(state);
+        selectedEntry.set(newGroup == 2 ? 16 + state : mode * 4 + (newGroup == 0 ? 0 : state + 1));
+        changed();
+    }
+    public OledResourceState current() { return resources.get(selectedEntry.get()); }
+    public void put(OledResourceState state) { resources.put(state.entry(), state); changed(); }
+    public void changed() { revision.set(revision.get() + 1); }
+    public void editBrightness(int value) {
+        if (value < 1 || value > 100) throw new IllegalArgumentException("brightness");
+        brightness.set(value); brightnessDirty = true; brightnessRevision++; simulate(1); changed();
+    }
+    public void readBrightness(int value) { if (!brightnessDirty) brightness.set(value); changed(); }
+    public void savedBrightness(int value) { if (brightness.get() == value) brightnessDirty = false; endPreview(); changed(); }
+    public boolean brightnessDirty() { return brightnessDirty; }
+    public long brightnessRevision() { return brightnessRevision; }
+    public void savedBrightness(int value, long edit) {
+        if (edit == brightnessRevision) savedBrightness(value); else endPreview();
+    }
+    public int effect(int mode, int ai) { return effects[mode][ai]; }
+    public void editEffect(int mode, int ai, int effect) {
+        if (effect < 0 || effect > 16) throw new IllegalArgumentException("effect");
+        effects[mode][ai] = effect; effectDirty[mode][ai] = true; effectRevisions[mode][ai]++; simulate(effect); changed();
+    }
+    public void readEffect(int mode, int ai, int effect) { if (!effectDirty[mode][ai]) effects[mode][ai] = effect; changed(); }
+    public void savedEffect(int mode, int ai, int effect) { if (effects[mode][ai] == effect) effectDirty[mode][ai] = false; endPreview(); changed(); }
+    public boolean effectDirty(int mode, int ai) { return effectDirty[mode][ai]; }
+    public long effectRevision(int mode, int ai) { return effectRevisions[mode][ai]; }
+    public void savedEffect(int mode, int ai, int effect, long edit) {
+        if (edit == effectRevisions[mode][ai]) savedEffect(mode, ai, effect); else endPreview();
+    }
+    public void simulate(int effect) {
+        simulatedEffect.set(effect); simulatedBrightness.set(brightness.get()); previewSimulation = false; changed();
+    }
+    public void previewAccepted(int effect, int level) {
+        previewAccepted(effect, level, System.nanoTime());
+    }
+    public void previewAccepted(int effect, int level, long acceptedAt) {
+        simulatedEffect.set(effect); simulatedBrightness.set(level); devicePreview.set(true);
+        previewSimulation = true;
+        previewUntilNanos.set(acceptedAt + 10_000_000_000L); changed();
+    }
+    public void endPreview() {
+        if (previewSimulation) simulatedEffect.set(-1);
+        previewSimulation = false;
+        devicePreview.set(false); previewUntilNanos.set(0);
+    }
+}

### src/main/java/com/example/ahakey/model/OledResourceState.java
--- start/src/main/java/com/example/ahakey/model/OledResourceState.java
+++ current/src/main/java/com/example/ahakey/model/OledResourceState.java
@@ -0,0 +1,112 @@
+package com.example.ahakey.model;
+
+import com.example.ahakey.protocol.AhaKeyProtocol;
+import java.util.zip.CRC32;
+
+/** Local intent and device observations are deliberately independent. */
+public record OledResourceState(int entry, Asset asset, LocalStatus localStatus,
+                                Metadata device, boolean deviceKnown, boolean inFlight,
+                                boolean commitUncertain, String notice) {
+    public enum LocalStatus { EMPTY, DRAFT, FAILED, APPLIED }
+
+    public record Asset(byte[] bytes, int frames, long crc) {
+        public Asset {
+            if (frames < 1 || frames > 12 || bytes.length != frames * AhaKeyProtocol.OLED_FRAME_SLOT_SIZE)
+                throw new IllegalArgumentException("Invalid OLED asset size");
+            CRC32 checksum = new CRC32();
+            checksum.update(bytes);
+            if (checksum.getValue() != crc) throw new IllegalArgumentException("Invalid OLED asset CRC");
+            for (int f = 0; f < frames; f++) {
+                for (int i = AhaKeyProtocol.OLED_FRAME_BYTES; i < AhaKeyProtocol.OLED_FRAME_SLOT_SIZE; i++)
+                    if (bytes[f * AhaKeyProtocol.OLED_FRAME_SLOT_SIZE + i] != 0)
+                        throw new IllegalArgumentException("Invalid OLED padding");
+            }
+            bytes = bytes.clone();
+        }
+        @Override public byte[] bytes() { return bytes.clone(); }
+        public static Asset fromBytes(byte[] bytes) {
+            CRC32 crc = new CRC32();
+            crc.update(bytes);
+            return new Asset(bytes, bytes.length / AhaKeyProtocol.OLED_FRAME_SLOT_SIZE, crc.getValue());
+        }
+    }
+
+    public record Metadata(boolean valid, int writeState, int frames, long bytes, long crc) {
+        public Metadata {
+            if (writeState < 0 || writeState > 3 || valid != (writeState == 2) || crc < 0 || crc > 0xffffffffL
+                    || (valid && (frames < 1 || frames > 12 || bytes != (long) frames * AhaKeyProtocol.OLED_FRAME_SLOT_SIZE))
+                    || (!valid && (frames != 0 || bytes != 0 || crc != 0)))
+                throw new IllegalArgumentException("Inconsistent OLED metadata");
+        }
+        public boolean matches(Asset asset) {
+            return valid && asset != null && frames == asset.frames() && bytes == asset.bytes.length && crc == asset.crc();
+        }
+    }
+
+    public OledResourceState {
+        int cap = AhaKeyProtocol.resourceCapacity(entry);
+        if (asset != null && asset.frames() > cap) throw new IllegalArgumentException("OLED capacity");
+        if (device != null && device.frames() > cap) throw new IllegalArgumentException("OLED device capacity");
+        if (localStatus == null || (asset == null) != (localStatus == LocalStatus.EMPTY))
+            throw new IllegalArgumentException("OLED local state");
+        if (deviceKnown && device == null) throw new IllegalArgumentException("OLED device fact");
+        notice = notice == null ? "" : notice;
+    }
+
+    public static OledResourceState empty(int entry) {
+        return new OledResourceState(entry, null, LocalStatus.EMPTY, null, false, false, false, "");
+    }
+    public int capacity() { return AhaKeyProtocol.resourceCapacity(entry); }
+    public boolean canUpload() { return asset != null && !inFlight && (localStatus == LocalStatus.DRAFT || localStatus == LocalStatus.FAILED); }
+    public String targetName() {
+        String[] single = {"默认屏幕", "SINGLE · 工作中", "SINGLE · 等待中", "SINGLE · 已完成"};
+        String[] multi = {"运行中", "等待中", "已完成"};
+        return entry < 16 ? "Mode " + (entry / 4 + 1) + " · " + single[entry % 4] : "全局 MULTI · " + multi[entry - 16];
+    }
+    public OledResourceState replace(Asset value) {
+        return new OledResourceState(entry, value, LocalStatus.DRAFT, device, deviceKnown, false, false, "");
+    }
+    public OledResourceState clear() {
+        return new OledResourceState(entry, null, LocalStatus.EMPTY, device, deviceKnown, false, false, "");
+    }
+    public OledResourceState uploading() {
+        if (!canUpload()) throw new IllegalStateException("没有可上传的草稿。");
+        return new OledResourceState(entry, asset, localStatus, device, false, true, false, "");
+    }
+    public OledResourceState failed(String reason, boolean uncertainCommit) {
+        return new OledResourceState(entry, asset, asset == null ? LocalStatus.EMPTY : LocalStatus.FAILED,
+                device, false, false, uncertainCommit, reason);
+    }
+    public OledResourceState committing() {
+        return new OledResourceState(entry, asset, localStatus, device, false, true, true, "设备保存结果未确认。");
+    }
+    public OledResourceState applied() {
+        if (asset == null) throw new IllegalStateException("Missing asset");
+        return new OledResourceState(entry, asset, LocalStatus.APPLIED,
+                new Metadata(true, 2, asset.frames(), asset.bytes.length, asset.crc()), true, false, false, "");
+    }
+    public OledResourceState unknown() {
+        return new OledResourceState(entry, asset, localStatus, device, false, inFlight, commitUncertain, notice);
+    }
+    public OledResourceState merge(Metadata value) {
+        LocalStatus status = localStatus;
+        String message = notice;
+        if (asset != null && localStatus == LocalStatus.APPLIED && !value.matches(asset)) {
+            status = LocalStatus.DRAFT;
+            message = "本机预览和当前键盘不一致，已作为草稿保留。";
+        } else if (commitUncertain && value.matches(asset)) {
+            status = LocalStatus.APPLIED;
+            message = "已应用到 " + targetName() + "。";
+        }
+        return new OledResourceState(entry, asset, status, value, true, false, false, message);
+    }
+    public String statusText() {
+        if (inFlight) return "上传中...";
+        return switch (localStatus) {
+            case DRAFT -> "草稿";
+            case FAILED -> commitUncertain ? "保存结果未确认" : "上次失败";
+            case APPLIED -> deviceKnown ? "已应用" : "已应用（待核对）";
+            case EMPTY -> deviceKnown ? (device.valid() ? "设备已有" : "空") : "设备状态未知";
+        };
+    }
+}

 import com.example.ahakey.model.AiRuntimeSlotState;
 
 public class AhaKeyProtocol {
+    public static final byte CMD_DISPLAY_READ = (byte) 0x88;
+    public static final byte CMD_DISPLAY_PREVIEW = (byte) 0x89;
+    public static final byte CMD_RESOURCE_BEGIN = (byte) 0x8A;
+    public static final byte CMD_RESOURCE_CHUNK = (byte) 0x8B;
+    public static final byte CMD_RESOURCE_COMMIT = (byte) 0x8C;
+    public static final byte CMD_DISPLAY_SAVE = (byte) 0x8E;
+    public static final int RESOURCE_COUNT = 19;
+    public static final int RESOURCE_CHUNK_BYTES = 48;
+
+    public static int resourceCapacity(int entry) {
+        requireRange(entry, 0, 18, "entry");
+        return entry < 16 && entry % 4 == 0 ? 8 : 12;
+    }
+
+    public static byte[] displayRead(int kind, int... selectors) {
+        requireRange(kind, 0, 3, "read kind");
+        int count = kind == 0 ? 0 : kind == 1 ? 2 : 1;
+        if (selectors.length != count) throw new IllegalArgumentException("selectors");
+        if (kind == 1) {
+            requireRange(selectors[0], 0, 3, "mode");
+            requireRange(selectors[1], 0, 8, "AI state");
+        } else if (kind > 1) requireRange(selectors[0], 0, 18, "entry");
+        byte[] data = new byte[count + 1];
+        data[0] = (byte) kind;
+        for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) data[i + 1] = (byte) selectors[i];
+        return buildFrame(CMD_DISPLAY_READ, data);
+    }
+
+    public static byte[] previewBrightness(int brightness) {
+        requireRange(brightness, 1, 100, "brightness");
+        return buildFrame(CMD_DISPLAY_PREVIEW, new byte[]{0, (byte) brightness});
+    }
+
+    public static byte[] previewLight(int effect, int brightness) {
+        requireRange(effect, 1, 16, "effect");
+        requireRange(brightness, 1, 100, "brightness");
+        return buildFrame(CMD_DISPLAY_PREVIEW, new byte[]{1, (byte) effect, (byte) brightness});
+    }
+
+    public static byte[] saveDisplayBrightness(int brightness) {
+        requireRange(brightness, 1, 100, "brightness");
+        return buildFrame(CMD_DISPLAY_SAVE, new byte[]{0, (byte) brightness});
+    }
+
+    public static byte[] saveDisplayLight(int mode, int ai, int effect) {
+        requireRange(mode, 0, 3, "mode");
+        requireRange(ai, 0, 8, "AI state");
+        requireRange(effect, 0, 16, "effect");
+        return buildFrame(CMD_DISPLAY_SAVE, new byte[]{1, (byte) mode, (byte) ai, (byte) effect});
+    }
+
+    public static byte[] resourceBegin(int entry, int frames, long crc) {
+        requireRange(frames, 1, resourceCapacity(entry), "frames");
+        if (crc < 0 || crc > 0xffffffffL) throw new IllegalArgumentException("CRC32");
+        java.nio.ByteBuffer data = java.nio.ByteBuffer.allocate(11).order(java.nio.ByteOrder.LITTLE_ENDIAN);
+        data.put((byte) entry).putInt(frames * OLED_FRAME_SLOT_SIZE).putShort((short) frames).putInt((int) crc);
+        return buildFrame(CMD_RESOURCE_BEGIN, data.array());
+    }
+
+    public static byte[] resourceChunk(int entry, int offset, byte[] payload) {
+        int capacity = resourceCapacity(entry) * OLED_FRAME_SLOT_SIZE;
+        requireRange(payload.length, 1, RESOURCE_CHUNK_BYTES, "chunk length");
+        requireRange(offset, 0, capacity - payload.length, "offset");
+        java.nio.ByteBuffer data = java.nio.ByteBuffer.allocate(6 + payload.length).order(java.nio.ByteOrder.LITTLE_ENDIAN);
+        data.put((byte) entry).putInt(offset).put((byte) payload.length).put(payload);
+        return buildFrame(CMD_RESOURCE_CHUNK, data.array());
+    }
+
+    public static byte[] resourceCommit(int entry) {
+        resourceCapacity(entry);
+        return buildFrame(CMD_RESOURCE_COMMIT, new byte[]{(byte) entry});
+    }
+
+    private static void requireRange(int value, int min, int max, String field) {
+        if (value < min || value > max) throw new IllegalArgumentException(field + ": " + value);
+    }
     private static final byte[] HEADER = {(byte) 0xAA, (byte) 0xBB};
     private static final byte[] TRAILER = {(byte) 0xCC, (byte) 0xDD};

+    public static boolean isDisplayCapabilityResponse(byte[] frame) {
+        if (!isValidFrame(frame) || frame.length < 6 || frame[2] != CMD_FIRMWARE_CATALOG_KEY) return false;
+        int offset = frame[3] == 0 ? 4 : 3;
+        byte[] key = CP4C_CATALOG_KEY.getBytes(java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.US_ASCII);
+        if (frame.length - 2 - offset < key.length) return false;
+        for (byte value : key) if (frame[offset++] != value) return false;
+        while (offset < frame.length - 2) if (frame[offset++] != 0) return false;
+        return true;
+    }

 import java.util.Arrays;
 
 public final class AhaKeyResponseParser {
+    public record DisplayRead(int kind, int selector, int aiState, int value, int writeState,
+                              int frames, long bytes, long crc) { }
+
+    public static DisplayRead parseDisplayRead(byte[] frame, int kind, int... selectors) {
+        CommandResponse response = parseCommandResponse(frame);
+        if (response == null || response.cmd() != AhaKeyProtocol.CMD_DISPLAY_READ || response.status() != 0)
+            return null;
+        int length = kind == 0 ? 8 : kind == 1 || kind == 2 ? 10 : kind == 3 ? 18 : -1;
+        if (frame.length != length || (frame[4] & 255) != kind) return null;
+        if (selectors.length != (kind == 0 ? 0 : kind == 1 ? 2 : 1)) return null;
+        for (int i = 0; i < selectors.length; i++) if ((frame[5 + i] & 255) != selectors[i]) return null;
+        int value = frame[kind == 1 ? 7 : kind == 2 ? 6 : 5] & 255;
+        if (kind == 0 && (value < 1 || value > 100)) return null;
+        if (kind == 2 && (value > 1 || (frame[7] & 255) > 3 || (value == 1) != (frame[7] == 2))) return null;
+        if (kind == 3) {
+            java.nio.ByteBuffer data = java.nio.ByteBuffer.wrap(frame).order(java.nio.ByteOrder.LITTLE_ENDIAN);
+            int frames = data.getShort(6) & 65535;
+            long bytes = Integer.toUnsignedLong(data.getInt(8));
+            long crc = Integer.toUnsignedLong(data.getInt(12));
+            if (frames > AhaKeyProtocol.resourceCapacity(selectors[0]) || bytes != (long) frames * AhaKeyProtocol.OLED_FRAME_SLOT_SIZE
+                    || (frames == 0 && crc != 0)) return null;
+            return new DisplayRead(kind, selectors[0], -1, 0, -1, frames, bytes, crc);
+        }
+        return new DisplayRead(kind, kind == 0 ? -1 : selectors[0], kind == 1 ? selectors[1] : -1,
+                value, kind == 2 ? frame[7] & 255 : -1, 0, 0, 0);
+    }
+
+    public static boolean isDisplayAck(byte[] frame, byte opcode) {
+        return AhaKeyProtocol.isValidFrame(frame) && frame.length == 6 && frame[2] == opcode
+                && (frame[3] == 0 || frame[3] == 1);
+    }
+
+    // -1: legacy opcode; 0: incomplete; -2: malformed; positive: exact CP2 frame length.
+    public static int displayReplyLength(byte[] data, int start, int available) {
+        if (available < 3) return 0;
+        int cmd = data[start + 2] & 255;
+        if (cmd != 0x88 && cmd != 0x89 && cmd != 0x8A && cmd != 0x8B && cmd != 0x8C && cmd != 0x8E) return -1;
+        if (available < 4) return 0;
+        int status = data[start + 3] & 255;
+        if (status > 1) return -2;
+        int length = 6;
+        if (cmd == 0x88 && status == 0) {
+            if (available < 5) return 0;
+            length = switch (data[start + 4] & 255) { case 0 -> 8; case 1, 2 -> 10; case 3 -> 18; default -> -2; };
+            if (length < 0) return length;
+        }
+        if (available < length) return 0;
+        return data[start + length - 2] == (byte) 0xCC && data[start + length - 1] == (byte) 0xDD ? length : -2;
+    }
     public record CommandResponse(byte cmd, byte status, byte[] payload) {
     }

-    public ApplyPlan prepareApply(boolean codexHookLatest, boolean cursorHookLatest) {
+        return handleHookEventResult(platform, state, payload) == Delivery.SENT;
+    }
+
+    public enum Delivery { SENT, PENDING, IGNORED, FAILED }
+    private static final long FACT_TTL_NANOS = java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.MINUTES.toNanos(5);
+    private record RuntimeFact(IDEState state, long observedNanos, long connection) {
+        boolean fresh(long currentConnection) {
+            return connection == currentConnection && System.nanoTime() - observedNanos < FACT_TTL_NANOS;
+        }
+    }
+    private final Map<String, RuntimeFact> liveFacts = new java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap<>();
+    // Display occupancy is distinct from a stable session identity; anonymous tools remain owners.
+    private record SingleOwner(String provider, String sessionId) {}
+    private SingleOwner singleOwner;
+    private long singleOwnerAt;
+    private long singleOwnerConnection = -1;
+
+    public Delivery handleHookEventResult(String platform, IDEState state, JsonNode payload) {
+        String id = "Cursor".equals(platform)
+                ? firstNonBlank(text(payload, "conversation_id"), text(payload, "conversationId"),
+                    text(payload, "composerId"), text(payload, "composer_id"))
+                : firstNonBlank(text(payload, "session_id"), text(payload, "sessionId"));
+        String transcript = "Codex".equals(platform)
+                ? firstNonBlank(text(payload, "transcript_path"), text(payload, "transcriptPath")) : "";
+        var source = "Codex".equals(platform)
+                ? codexDiscovery.eventSource(Path.of(codexDataDir), id)
+                : CodexSessionDiscoveryService.EventSource.UNKNOWN;
+        Delivery result;
+        String matchedId = "";
+        synchronized (snapshotSendLock) {
+            liveFacts.entrySet().removeIf(entry -> !entry.getValue().fresh(bleManager.connectionGeneration()));
+            if (source == CodexSessionDiscoveryService.EventSource.BACKGROUND) {
+                result = Delivery.IGNORED;
+            } else if (displayMode.get() == AiRuntimeDisplayMode.SINGLE) {
+                String identity = id.isBlank() ? "" : platform + ":" + id;
+                boolean terminal = state == IDEState.TASK_COMPLETED || state == IDEState.STOP || state == IDEState.SESSION_END;
+                // A trusted terminal fact must survive even when another session owns SINGLE.
+                if (!id.isBlank()) liveFacts.put(identity,
+                        new RuntimeFact(state, System.nanoTime(), bleManager.connectionGeneration()));
+                SingleOwner eventOwner = new SingleOwner(platform, id);
+                if (singleOwnerConnection != bleManager.connectionGeneration()
+                        || System.nanoTime() - singleOwnerAt >= FACT_TTL_NANOS) singleOwner = null;
+                if (terminal && singleOwner != null && !singleOwner.equals(eventOwner)) {
+                    result = Delivery.IGNORED;
+                } else {
+                    if (!terminal) {
+                        singleOwner = eventOwner;
+                        singleOwnerAt = System.nanoTime();
+                        singleOwnerConnection = bleManager.connectionGeneration();
+                    }
+                    try {
+                        result = bleManager.sendRuntimeState((byte) state.getCode()) ? Delivery.SENT
+                                : bleManager.isConnected() ? Delivery.PENDING : Delivery.FAILED;
+                    } catch (Exception e) { result = Delivery.FAILED; }
+                }
+            } else if (!isDeviceSupported() || (!"Codex".equals(platform) && !"Cursor".equals(platform))) {
+                result = Delivery.IGNORED;
+            } else {
+                int matched = -1;
+                for (int i = 0; i < appliedSlots.length; i++) {
+                    if (matches(appliedSlots[i], platform, id, transcript)) {
+                        if (matched >= 0) { matched = -2; break; }
+                        matched = i;
+                    }
+                }
+                if (matched < 0) result = Delivery.IGNORED;
+                else {
+                    AiRuntimeSlotBinding slot = appliedSlots[matched];
+                    matchedId = slot.getStableId();
+                    liveFacts.put(slot.getProvider() + ":" + slot.getStableId(),
+                            new RuntimeFact(state, System.nanoTime(), bleManager.connectionGeneration()));
+                    slot.setLastIdeState(state.name());
+                    AiRuntimeSlotBinding draft = slots.get(matched);
+                    if (Objects.equals(draft.getStableId(), slot.getStableId()) && Objects.equals(draft.getProvider(), slot.getProvider()))
+                        draft.setLastIdeState(state.name());
+                    snapshotRevision.incrementAndGet();
+                    try { result = sendAppliedSnapshot(bleManager.connectionGeneration()) ? Delivery.SENT : Delivery.PENDING; }
+                    catch (Exception e) { result = Delivery.FAILED; }
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        // Never log the original Hook input, transcript contents, cwd or chat text.
+        logger.info("Runtime event platform={} session={} matchedSession={} state={} source={} result={} connection={}",
+                platform, diagnosticId(id), diagnosticId(matchedId), state, source, result, bleManager.connectionGeneration());
+        return result;
+    }
+
+    private String diagnosticId(String id) {
+        return id.matches("[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,128}") ? id : id.isBlank() ? "absent" : "redacted";
+    }
+
+    private void applyLiveFact(AiRuntimeSlotBinding slot) {
+        RuntimeFact fact = liveFacts.get(slot.getProvider() + ":" + slot.getStableId());
+        slot.setLastIdeState(fact != null && fact.fresh(bleManager.connectionGeneration()) ? fact.state().name() : "");
+    }

+            boolean notify;
+            commandLock.lock();

+                notify = displayLease == this && generation == connectionGeneration && !displayWriteUncertain;
+                closed = true; if (displayLease == this) displayLease = null; responseReady.signalAll();
+            }
+            finally { commandLock.unlock(); }
+            return notify;
+        }

+    public DisplayLease openDisplayLease(boolean writing, long expectedGeneration) throws IOException {
+        commandLock.lock();
+        try {
+            if (expectedGeneration != connectionGeneration) throw new IOException("键盘连接已改变。");
+            if (!isConnected || !cachedStatus.isConnected()) throw new IOException("键盘连接已断开。");
+            if (displayLease != null || legacyWaiters != 0) throw new IOException("设备正在处理其他事务。");
+            if (writing && (!isDisplayAdmitted() || displayWriteUncertain))
+                throw new IOException(displayWriteUncertain ? "设备保存结果未确认，请重新连接后同步。" : "当前连接尚未识别到支持的固件版本。");
+            if (!writing && displayReadUncertain) throw new IOException("上次读取结果未确认，请重新连接后同步。");
+            displayLease = new DisplayLease(writing);
+            return displayLease;
+        } finally { commandLock.unlock(); }
+    }

+    public byte[] exchangeDisplay(DisplayLease lease, byte[] request) throws Exception {
+        long queuedAt = System.nanoTime();
+        commandLock.lockInterruptibly();
+        long acquiredAt = System.nanoTime();
+        long sentAt = 0;
+        String outcome = "NOT_SENT";
+        boolean sent = false;
+        try {
+            checkDisplayLease(lease);
+            requireCapability();
+            int opcode = request[2] & 255;
+            if (opcode != 0x88 && opcode != 0x89 && opcode != 0x8A && opcode != 0x8B && opcode != 0x8C && opcode != 0x8E)
+                throw new IllegalArgumentException("CP2 opcode");
+            if (opcode != 0x88 && (!lease.writing || displayWriteUncertain || !isDisplayAdmitted())) throw new IOException("显示配置写入未准入。");
+            if (opcode == 0x88 && displayReadUncertain) throw new IOException("读取结果未确认，请重新连接。");
+            displayExpected = request[2];
+            displayReply = null;
+            sent = true;
+            outcome = "WRITE_ATTEMPTED_UNKNOWN";
+            if (lease.usb) usbTransport.sendCommand(request);
+            else writePacket(BleTcpPacket.WRITE_COMMAND, request);
+            sentAt = System.nanoTime();
+            outcome = "ACK_UNKNOWN";
+            long deadline = System.nanoTime() + TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS.toNanos(RESPONSE_TIMEOUT_MS);
+            while (true) {
+                checkDisplayLease(lease);
+                if (displayReply != null) {
+                    byte[] reply = displayReply;
+                    displayReply = null;
+                    if (AhaKeyResponseParser.displayReplyLength(reply, 0, reply.length) != reply.length)
+                        throw new IOException("显示配置响应格式无效。");
+                    outcome = reply[3] == 0 ? "ACK_OK" : "ACK_FAIL";
+                    return reply;
+                }
+                long remaining = deadline - System.nanoTime();
+                if (remaining <= 0) throw new IOException("设备保存或读取结果未确认，请重新连接后同步。");
+                responseReady.awaitNanos(Math.min(remaining, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS.toNanos(100)));
+            }
+        } catch (Exception e) {
+            if (sent && lease.generation == connectionGeneration) {
+                if (request[2] == AhaKeyProtocol.CMD_DISPLAY_READ) displayReadUncertain = true;
+                else displayWriteUncertain = true;
+            }
+            throw e;
+        } finally {
+            long finishedAt = System.nanoTime();
+            displayExpected = 0;
+            commandLock.unlock();
+            int opcode = request[2] & 255;
+            if (opcode >= 0x8A && opcode <= 0x8C) {
+                long offset = opcode == 0x8B ? Integer.toUnsignedLong(java.nio.ByteBuffer.wrap(request, 4, 4)
+                        .order(java.nio.ByteOrder.LITTLE_ENDIAN).getInt()) : 0;
+                logger.info("CP2 exchange connection={} transport={} opcode={} entry={} offset={} outcome={} queueUs={} writeUs={} ackUs={}",
+                        lease.generation, lease.usb ? "USB" : "BLE", opcode, request[3] & 255, offset, outcome,
+                        (acquiredAt - queuedAt) / 1000, sentAt == 0 ? -1 : (sentAt - acquiredAt) / 1000,
+                        sentAt == 0 ? -1 : (finishedAt - sentAt) / 1000);
+            }
+        }
+    }

+                || lease.usb != usbTransport.isOpen()) throw new IOException("键盘连接已改变，本次事务已停止。");
+    }

+    public boolean sendRuntimeState(byte state) throws IOException {
+        commandLock.lock();
+        try {
+            if (!isConnected || !cachedStatus.isConnected()) return false;
+            deferredRuntimeState = state;
+            deferredRuntimeAt = System.nanoTime();
+            if (displayLease != null || displayWriteUncertain) return false;
+            sendCommand(AhaKeyProtocol.updateState(state));
+            deferredRuntimeState = null;
+            return true;
+        } finally { commandLock.unlock(); }
+    }
+    private void flushDeferredRuntimeState() {
+        commandLock.lock();
+        try {
+            if (deferredRuntimeState != null && System.nanoTime() - deferredRuntimeAt >= TimeUnit.MINUTES.toNanos(5))
+                deferredRuntimeState = null;
+            if (displayLease != null || displayWriteUncertain) return;
+            if (deferredRuntimeState != null && isConnected && cachedStatus.isConnected()) {
+                // Retry the original fact; only a newly received event may advance its TTL.
+                sendCommand(AhaKeyProtocol.updateState(deferredRuntimeState));
+                deferredRuntimeState = null;
+            }
+        } catch (IOException e) { logger.debug("Runtime state remains pending: {}", e.getMessage()); }
+        finally { commandLock.unlock(); }
+    }

     private static final Logger logger = LoggerFactory.getLogger(CodexSessionDiscoveryService.class);
     private final ObjectMapper mapper = new ObjectMapper();
 
+    public enum EventSource { USER, BACKGROUND, UNKNOWN }
+
+    /** Bounded, read-only metadata lookup. Missing/new sessions remain UNKNOWN, never background. */
+    public EventSource eventSource(Path dataDir, String stableId) {
+        if (dataDir == null || stableId == null || stableId.isBlank()) return EventSource.UNKNOWN;
+        Path db = dataDir.resolve("state_5.sqlite");
+        if (!Files.isRegularFile(db)) return EventSource.UNKNOWN;
+        try (Connection connection = DriverManager.getConnection("jdbc:sqlite:" + db.toUri() + "?mode=ro");
+             var query = connection.prepareStatement("select source, thread_source from threads where id = ? limit 1")) {
+            query.setString(1, stableId);
+            query.setQueryTimeout(1);
+            try (ResultSet rs = query.executeQuery()) {
+                if (!rs.next()) return EventSource.UNKNOWN;
+                String source = value(rs.getString(1));
+                String kind = value(rs.getString(2));
+                if ("composer_predictions".equals(kind) || "guardian_review".equals(kind)) return EventSource.BACKGROUND;
+                if (source.startsWith("{")) {
+                    String other = mapper.readTree(source).path("subagent").path("other").asText("");
+                    if ("guardian".equals(other) || "composer_predictions".equals(other)) return EventSource.BACKGROUND;
+                }
+                return "user".equals(kind) ? EventSource.USER : EventSource.UNKNOWN;
+            }
+        } catch (Exception e) {
+            logger.debug("Runtime source metadata unavailable: {}", e.getClass().getSimpleName());
+            return EventSource.UNKNOWN;
+        }
+    }

+package com.example.ahakey.service;

+public final class DisplayConfigService implements AutoCloseable {
+    private static final org.slf4j.Logger logger = org.slf4j.LoggerFactory.getLogger(DisplayConfigService.class);
+    private final StudioState studio;
+    private final DisplayConfigState state;
+    private final BleManager ble;
+    private final OledResourceStore store;
+    private final ExecutorService worker = Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor(r -> {
+        Thread thread = new Thread(r, "display-config"); thread.setDaemon(true); return thread;
+    });
+    private volatile long loadGeneration;
+    private long operationGeneration;
+    private long observedConnection = -1;
+    private boolean wasConnected;
+    private boolean closed;

+    public DisplayConfigService(StudioState studio, BleManager ble) {
+        this(studio, ble, new OledResourceStore());
+    }
+    public DisplayConfigService(StudioState studio, BleManager ble, OledResourceStore store) {
+        this.studio = studio;
+        this.state = studio.displayConfig();
+        this.ble = ble;
+        this.store = Objects.requireNonNull(store);
+        state.select(studio.getSelectedMode().getIndex(), 0, 0);
+        Map<Integer, String> legacy = new HashMap<>();
+        for (ModeSlot mode : ModeSlot.values()) legacy.put(mode.getIndex() * 4, studio.getOledDraft(mode).getLocalAssetPath());
+        worker.execute(() -> {
+            try {
+                Map<Integer, OledResourceState> loaded = store.load();
+                List<String> warnings = new ArrayList<>();
+                for (var item : legacy.entrySet()) {
+                    if (item.getValue() == null || item.getValue().isBlank() || store.containsEntry(item.getKey())) continue;
+                    try {
+                        Path path = Path.of(item.getValue());
+                        if (!Files.isRegularFile(path)) continue;
+                        var asset = OledResourceState.Asset.fromBytes(OLEDFrameEncoder.paddedStream(OLEDFrameEncoder.importFile(path, 8)));
+                        var migrated = loaded.get(item.getKey()).replace(asset);
+                        store.save(migrated); loaded.put(item.getKey(), migrated);
+                    } catch (Exception e) { warnings.add("旧 OLED 草稿迁移失败：" + reason(e)); }
+                }
+                fx(() -> {
+                    loaded.values().forEach(state::put); state.ready.set(true);
+                    if (!warnings.isEmpty()) errorHandler.accept(String.join("\n", warnings));
+                    refresh();
+                });
+            } catch (Exception e) { fx(() -> error("本地资源库读取失败：" + reason(e))); }
+        });
+        studio.selectedModeProperty().addListener((o, a, b) -> {
+            state.select(b.getIndex(), state.group.get(), state.substate.get()); refresh();
+        });
+        studio.selectedPartProperty().addListener((o, a, b) -> refresh());
+        studio.modeSwitchingProperty().addListener((o, a, b) -> { updateWriteReady(); refresh(); });
+    }
+    public void setErrorHandler(Consumer<String> handler) { errorHandler = handler; }
+    public DisplayConfigState state() { return state; }
+
+    public void connectionChanged() {
+        long generation = ble.connectionGeneration();
+        boolean connected = ble.isConnected() && ble.getCachedStatus().isConnected();
+        if (generation != observedConnection || connected != wasConnected) {
+            if (observedConnection >= 0) state.message.set("键盘连接已改变，本地草稿已保留。");
+            observedConnection = generation; wasConnected = connected; loadGeneration++;
+            state.loading.set(false);
+            state.resources.replaceAll((entry, resource) -> resource.unknown());
+            state.endPreview(); state.changed();
+            if (connected) refresh();
+        }
+        updateWriteReady();
+    }
+    public void capabilityChanged() {
+        updateWriteReady(); refresh();
+    }
+    private void updateWriteReady() {
+        state.capabilityReady.set(ble.isDisplayAdmitted());
+        String identity = switch (ble.firmwareIdentity().state()) {
+            case READING -> "display.identity-reading";
+            case FAILED -> "display.identity-failed";
+            case UNSUPPORTED -> "display.identity-unsupported";
+            default -> "display.identity-unknown";
+        };
+        state.disabledReasonKey.set(!ble.isConnected() || !ble.getCachedStatus().isConnected() ? "display.identity-disconnected"
+                : !ble.isDisplayAdmitted() ? identity
+                : ble.isDisplayWriteUncertain() ? "display.recovery-required"
+                : state.busy.get() ? (studio.uploadingOledProperty().get() ? "display.upload-busy" : "display.operation-busy")
+                : studio.modeSwitchingProperty().get() ? "display.mode-pending" : "");
+        state.writeReady.set(ble.isDisplayAdmitted() && !ble.isDisplayWriteUncertain()
+                && !state.busy.get() && !studio.modeSwitchingProperty().get());
+    }
+
+    public void select(int group, int substate) { state.select(studio.getSelectedMode().getIndex(), group, substate); }
+    public void importFile(Path path, int entry) {
+        if (!state.ready.get() || state.busy.get()) return;
+        OledResourceState previous = state.resources.get(entry);
+        state.message.set("处理中...");
+        work(operation -> {
+            var asset = OledResourceState.Asset.fromBytes(OLEDFrameEncoder.paddedStream(OLEDFrameEncoder.importFile(path, previous.capacity())));
+            var next = previous.replace(asset);
+            store.save(next);
+            fx(() -> { state.put(next); state.message.set("草稿已保存。"); });
+        });
+    }
+    public void clear(OledResourceState frozen) {
+        if (!state.ready.get() || state.busy.get()) return;
+        var current = state.resources.get(frozen.entry());
+        if (current.asset() != frozen.asset()) { error("本地资源已变化，请重新确认清除目标。"); return; }
+        OledResourceState next = current.clear();
+        work(operation -> {
+            try { store.save(next); }
+            catch (OledResourceStore.CleanupFailure e) {
+                fx(() -> { state.put(next); error(reason(e)); }); return;
+            }
+            fx(() -> { state.put(next); state.message.set("已清除本地资源。"); });
+        });
+    }
+
+    public void upload(OledResourceState frozen) {
+        if (state.busy.get() || !state.writeReady.get() || !frozen.canUpload() || state.current() != frozen) return;
+        long connection = ble.connectionGeneration();
+        studio.uploadingOledProperty().set(true);
+        state.message.set("上传中...");
+        work(true, operation -> {
+            OledResourceState result;
+            try {
+                store.save(frozen.uploading());
+                fx(() -> state.put(frozen.uploading()));
+                OledUploadService.uploadResource(ble, connection, frozen.entry(), frozen.asset(),
+                        percent -> {}, () -> store.save(frozen.committing()),
+                        progress -> deviceFx(operation, () -> state.progress.set(progress.text())));
+                result = frozen.applied();
+            } catch (OledUploadService.UploadFailure e) {
+                result = frozen.failed(reason(e), e.commitUncertain());
+            } catch (Exception e) {
+                result = frozen.failed(reason(e), false);
+            }
+            OledResourceState finalResult = result;
+            try { store.save(result); }
+            catch (IOException e) {
+                fx(() -> error("设备操作结果已保留在当前界面，但本地记录保存失败：" + reason(e)));
+            }
+            fx(() -> {
+                state.put(connection == ble.connectionGeneration() && wasConnected ? finalResult : finalResult.unknown());
+                state.message.set(finalResult.localStatus() == OledResourceState.LocalStatus.APPLIED
+                        ? "已应用到 " + finalResult.targetName() + "。" : finalResult.notice());
+            });
+        });
+    }
+
+    public void refresh() {
+        long ticket = ++loadGeneration;
+        state.loading.set(false);
+        StudioPart part = studio.getSelectedPart();
+        if (state.busy.get() || studio.modeSwitchingProperty().get() || !wasConnected || !ble.isDisplayAdmitted() || (part != StudioPart.OLED && part != StudioPart.LIGHT_BAR)) return;
+        if (part == StudioPart.OLED && !state.ready.get()) return;
+        int mode = studio.getSelectedMode().getIndex();
+        long connection = ble.connectionGeneration();
+        Map<Integer, OledResourceState> captured = new HashMap<>(state.resources);
+        state.loading.set(true);
+        worker.execute(() -> {
+            try {
+                ble.readDisplayConfig(connection, lease -> {
+                if (ticket != loadGeneration) return;
+                if (part == StudioPart.OLED) {
+                    Map<Integer, OledResourceState> updates = new LinkedHashMap<>();
+                    int[] targets = {mode * 4, mode * 4 + 1, mode * 4 + 2, mode * 4 + 3, 16, 17, 18};
+                    for (int entry : targets) {
+                        if (ticket != loadGeneration) return;
+                        var basic = read(lease, 2, entry);
+                        var integrity = read(lease, 3, entry);
+                        var metadata = new OledResourceState.Metadata(basic.value() == 1, basic.writeState(), integrity.frames(), integrity.bytes(), integrity.crc());
+                        updates.put(entry, captured.get(entry).merge(metadata));
+                    }
+                    if (ticket != loadGeneration || connection != ble.connectionGeneration()) return;
+                    for (var resource : updates.values()) store.save(resource);
+                    fx(() -> {
+                        if (ticket != loadGeneration || connection != ble.connectionGeneration()) return;
+                        updates.values().forEach(state::put);
+                        state.message.set(updates.values().stream().map(OledResourceState::notice).filter(s -> !s.isBlank()).findFirst().orElse("已同步资源元数据。"));
+                    });
+                } else {
+                    int brightness = read(lease, 0).value();
+                    int[] effects = new int[9];
+                    for (int ai = 0; ai < 9; ai++) {
+                        if (ticket != loadGeneration) return;
+                        effects[ai] = read(lease, 1, mode, ai).value();
+                    }
+                    fx(() -> {
+                        if (ticket != loadGeneration || connection != ble.connectionGeneration()) return;
+                        state.readBrightness(brightness);
+                        for (int ai = 0; ai < 9; ai++) state.readEffect(mode, ai, effects[ai]);
+                        state.message.set("已同步灯光配置。");
+                    });
+                }
+                });
+            } catch (Exception e) {
+                fx(() -> { if (ticket == loadGeneration) { state.message.set("同步失败：" + reason(e)); state.resources.replaceAll((i, r) -> r.unknown()); state.changed(); } });
+            } finally { fx(() -> { if (ticket == loadGeneration) state.loading.set(false); updateWriteReady(); }); }
+        });
+    }
+    private AhaKeyResponseParser.DisplayRead read(BleManager.DisplayLease lease, int kind, int... selectors) throws Exception {
+        byte[] reply = ble.exchangeDisplay(lease, AhaKeyProtocol.displayRead(kind, selectors));
+        if (reply.length == 6 && reply[3] == 1) throw new OledUploadService.DeviceRejected();
+        var parsed = AhaKeyResponseParser.parseDisplayRead(reply, kind, selectors);
+        if (parsed == null) {
+            ble.invalidateDisplayRead(lease);
+            throw new IOException("显示配置响应目标或内容不匹配，请重新连接后同步。");
+        }
+        return parsed;
+    }
+    public void preview(Integer effect) {
+        if (!state.writeReady.get() || state.busy.get() || (effect != null && (effect < 1 || effect > 16))) return;
+        int brightness = state.brightness.get();
+        long connection = ble.connectionGeneration();
+        work(true, operation -> {
+            try (BleManager.DisplayLease lease = ble.openDisplayLease(true, connection)) {
+                OledUploadService.requireAck(ble.exchangeDisplay(lease, effect == null ? AhaKeyProtocol.previewBrightness(brightness)
+                        : AhaKeyProtocol.previewLight(effect, brightness)), AhaKeyProtocol.CMD_DISPLAY_PREVIEW);
+                long acceptedAt = System.nanoTime();
+                deviceFx(operation, () -> {
+                    if (System.nanoTime() - acceptedAt >= 10_000_000_000L) return;
+                    state.previewAccepted(effect == null ? 1 : effect, brightness, acceptedAt);
+                    state.message.set("设备预览中（10 秒）。");
+                });
+            }
+        });
+    }
+    public void saveBrightness() {
+        if (!state.writeReady.get() || state.busy.get()) return;
+        int brightness = state.brightness.get();
+        long edit = state.brightnessRevision();
+        long connection = ble.connectionGeneration();
+        work(true, operation -> {
+            try (BleManager.DisplayLease lease = ble.openDisplayLease(true, connection)) {
+                OledUploadService.requireAck(ble.exchangeDisplay(lease, AhaKeyProtocol.saveDisplayBrightness(brightness)), AhaKeyProtocol.CMD_DISPLAY_SAVE);
+                logger.info("Brightness ACK: connection={}, operation={}, value={}", connection, operation.id(), brightness);
+                deviceFx(operation, () -> { state.savedBrightness(brightness, edit); state.message.set("已保存全局亮度。"); });
+            }
+        });
+    }
+    public void saveModeLights() {
+        if (!state.writeReady.get() || state.busy.get()) return;
+        int mode = studio.getSelectedMode().getIndex();
+        int[] effects = new int[9];
+        long[] edits = new long[9];
+        for (int ai = 0; ai < 9; ai++) {
+            effects[ai] = state.effect(mode, ai);
+            edits[ai] = state.effectRevision(mode, ai);
+            if (effects[ai] < 0 || effects[ai] > 16) { error("存在未知灯效，请为该状态选择有效灯效后保存。"); return; }
+        }
+        long connection = ble.connectionGeneration();
+        work(true, operation -> {
+            int saved = 0;
+            try (BleManager.DisplayLease lease = ble.openDisplayLease(true, connection)) {
+                for (int ai = 0; ai < 9; ai++) {
+                    OledUploadService.requireAck(ble.exchangeDisplay(lease, AhaKeyProtocol.saveDisplayLight(mode, ai, effects[ai])), AhaKeyProtocol.CMD_DISPLAY_SAVE);
+                    int key = ai;
+                    saved++;
+                    logger.info("Light ACK: connection={}, operation={}, mode={}, ai={}, confirmed={}/9", connection, operation.id(), mode, key, saved);
+                    deviceFx(operation, () -> state.savedEffect(mode, key, effects[key], edits[key]));
+                }
+                deviceFx(operation, () -> state.message.set("已保存 Mode " + (mode + 1) + " 的 9 项灯效。"));
+            } catch (Exception e) { throw new IOException("已确认保存 " + saved + " / 9 项；第 " + (saved + 1) + " 项未确认，其余草稿保留。" + reason(e), e); }
+        });
+    }
+    private record Operation(long id, long connection, boolean device) {}
+    private interface Work { void run(Operation operation) throws Exception; }
+    private void work(Work work) {
+        work(false, work);
+    }
+    private boolean current(Operation operation) {
+        return operation.id() == operationGeneration && operation.connection() == ble.connectionGeneration()
+                && wasConnected && ble.isConnected() && ble.getCachedStatus().isConnected();
+    }
+    private void deviceFx(Operation operation, Runnable action) {
+        fx(() -> { if (current(operation)) action.run(); });
+    }
+    private void work(boolean device, Work work) {
+        Operation operation = new Operation(++operationGeneration, ble.connectionGeneration(), device);
+        loadGeneration++; state.loading.set(false); state.busy.set(true); state.progress.set(""); updateWriteReady();
+        worker.execute(() -> {
+            try { work.run(operation); }
+            catch (Exception e) {
+                logger.warn("Display operation ended: connection={}, operation={}, reason={}", operation.connection(), operation.id(), reason(e));
+                fx(() -> {
+                    if (operation.device() && !current(operation)) return;
+                    if (ble.isDisplayWriteUncertain()) state.endPreview(); error(reason(e));
+                });
+            }
+            finally { fx(() -> {
+                if (operation.id() != operationGeneration) return;
+                boolean wasUpload = studio.uploadingOledProperty().get();
+                state.busy.set(false); studio.uploadingOledProperty().set(false); updateWriteReady();
+                if (wasUpload && state.current().localStatus() != OledResourceState.LocalStatus.APPLIED) refresh();
+            }); }
+        });
+    }
+    private void error(String message) { state.message.set(message); errorHandler.accept(message); }
+    private static String reason(Exception e) { return e.getMessage() == null ? e.getClass().getSimpleName() : e.getMessage(); }
+    private void fx(Runnable runnable) { Platform.runLater(() -> { if (!closed) runnable.run(); }); }
+    @Override public void close() { closed = true; loadGeneration++; worker.shutdownNow(); }
+}

+package com.example.ahakey.service;
+
+/** CP2 psk_ws2812.c / display_preview.c, 50 ms ticks, reset to firmware static initial phases. */
+public final class FirmwareLightSimulator {
+    private int position, direction = 1, hue, breath, fade = 1, ripple, comet, cometDir = 1;
+    private int scan, scanDir = 1, pulse, warn, success, think, low, charge, approval;
+    private final int[][] pixels = new int[8][4];
+
+    public int[] step(int effect, int brightness) {
+        if (effect < 0 || effect > 16 || brightness < 1 || brightness > 100) throw new IllegalArgumentException("light parameters");
+        for (int[] pixel : pixels) java.util.Arrays.fill(pixel, 0);
+        switch (effect) {
+            case 0 -> { }
+            case 1 -> {
+                for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
+                    int distance = Math.abs(i - position);
+                    if (distance <= 2) set(i, 0x10, 0x20, 0x80, distance == 0 ? 160 : distance == 1 ? 55 : 30);
+                }
+                move();
+            }
+            case 2, 3, 4 -> {
+                for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
+                    int h = (i * 32 + hue) & 255, region = h / 43, remainder = (h - region * 43) * 6;
+                    int alpha = 120;
+                    if (effect == 2) { int distance = Math.abs(i - position); alpha = distance == 0 ? 150 : distance == 1 ? 55 : distance == 2 ? 30 : 1; }
+                    switch (region) {
+                        case 0 -> set(i, 120, remainder / 2, 0, alpha);
+                        case 1 -> set(i, (255 - remainder) / 2, 120, 0, alpha);
+                        case 2 -> set(i, 0, 120, remainder / 2, alpha);
+                        case 3 -> set(i, 0, (255 - remainder) / 2, 120, alpha);
+                        case 4 -> set(i, remainder / 2, 0, 120, alpha);
+                        default -> set(i, 120, 0, (255 - remainder) / 2, alpha);
+                    }
+                }
+                hue = (hue - (effect == 4 ? 2 : 10)) & 255;
+                if (effect == 2) move();
+            }
+            case 5 -> {
+                for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) set(i, 0x10, 0x20, 0x80, breath);
+                breath = (breath + fade * 8) & 255;
+                if (breath >= 140) { breath = 140; fade = -1; }
+                else if (breath <= 20) { breath = 20; fade = 1; }
+            }
+            case 6 -> {
+                int[] alpha = {1, 15, 60, 130};
+                for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) set(i, 0x10, 0x20, 0x80, alpha[Math.min(i, 7 - i)]);
+            }
+            case 7 -> {
+                for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
+                    int wave = ripple - Math.min(Math.abs(i - 3), Math.abs(i - 4)) * 28;
+                    if (wave >= 0 && wave < 80) set(i, 8, 0x50, 0x60, wave < 32 ? wave * 4 : (80 - wave) * 2);
+                }
+                ripple += 12; if (ripple >= 150) ripple = 0;
+            }
+            case 8 -> {
+                int[] tail = {160, 90, 45, 20, 8};
+                for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) if (Math.abs(i - comet) < 5) set(i, 0x80, 0x28, 0x10, tail[Math.abs(i - comet)]);
+                comet += cometDir; if (comet >= 7) { comet = 7; cometDir = -1; } else if (comet == 0) cometDir = 1;
+            }
+            case 9 -> {
+                set(scan, 0x40, 0x40, 0x50, 140);
+                if (scan < 7) set(scan + 1, 8, 0x50, 0x60, 80);
+                if (scan > 0) set(scan - 1, 8, 0x50, 0x60, 35);
+                scan += scanDir; if (scan >= 7) { scan = 7; scanDir = -1; } else if (scan == 0) scanDir = 1;
+            }
+            case 10 -> {
+                int core = 35 + triangle(pulse) / 4;
+                set(3, 0x10, 0x28, 0x80, core); set(4, 0x10, 0x28, 0x80, core);
+                set(2, 0x10, 0x28, 0x80, core / 3); set(5, 0x10, 0x28, 0x80, core / 3);
+                pulse = (pulse + 6) & 255;
+            }
+            case 11 -> {
+                boolean on = warn % 18 < 5 || (warn + 8) % 18 < 3;
+                for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) if (on) set(i, 0x80, i % 2 == 1 ? 0x10 : 0x30, 0, 150); else set(i, 0x18, 0, 0, 15);
+                warn = (warn + 1) & 255;
+            }
+            case 12 -> {
+                for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
+                    if (i <= success) set(i, 8, 0x70, 0x24, 100);
+                    if (i == success) set(i, 0x30, 0x70, 0x30, 120);
+                }
+                success++; if (success >= 13) success = 0;
+            }
+            case 13 -> {
+                for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) set(i, 8, 0x20, 0x80, 20 + triangle(think + i * 24) / 4);
+                think = (think + 5) & 255;
+            }
+            case 14 -> {
+                boolean edge = low % 24 < 8;
+                for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) if (i == 0 || i == 7) set(i, 0x80, 0, 0, edge ? 150 : 25); else set(i, 0x18, 0, 0, edge ? 20 : 8);
+                low = (low + 1) & 255;
+            }
+            case 15 -> {
+                int fill = charge % 12;
+                for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) if (i < fill) set(i, 8, 0x70, 0x28, 100); else set(i, 0, 0x18, 8, 12);
+                if (fill < 8) set(fill, 0x30, 0x70, 0x30, 120);
+                charge = (charge + 1) & 255;
+            }
+            case 16 -> {
+                for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) set(i, 0x80, 0x30, 8, 30 + triangle(approval) / 4);
+                if (approval % 64 < 10) { set(3, 0x70, 0x60, 0x30, 120); set(4, 0x70, 0x60, 0x30, 120); }
+                approval = (approval + 4) & 255;
+            }
+        }
+        int[] output = new int[8];
+        for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
+            int[] p = pixels[i];
+            int level = p[3] == 0 ? brightness : (p[3] * brightness) >> 8;
+            output[i] = ((p[0] * level >> 8) << 16) | ((p[1] * level >> 8) << 8) | (p[2] * level >> 8);
+        }
+        return output;
+    }
+    private int triangle(int phase) { phase &= 255; return phase < 128 ? phase * 2 : (255 - phase) * 2; }
+    private void set(int i, int r, int g, int b, int alpha) { pixels[i][0] = r; pixels[i][1] = g; pixels[i][2] = b; pixels[i][3] = alpha; }
+    private void move() { position += direction; if (position >= 7) { position = 7; direction = -1; } else if (position <= 0) { position = 0; direction = 1; } }
+}

### src/main/java/com/example/ahakey/service/OledResourceStore.java
--- start/src/main/java/com/example/ahakey/service/OledResourceStore.java
+++ current/src/main/java/com/example/ahakey/service/OledResourceStore.java
@@ -0,0 +1,140 @@
+package com.example.ahakey.service;
+
+import com.example.ahakey.model.OledResourceState;
+import com.example.ahakey.model.OledResourceState.Asset;
+import com.example.ahakey.model.OledResourceState.LocalStatus;
+import com.example.ahakey.model.OledResourceState.Metadata;
+import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JsonNode;
+import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;
+import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.node.ObjectNode;
+import java.io.IOException;
+import java.nio.ByteBuffer;
+import java.nio.channels.FileChannel;
+import java.nio.file.Files;
+import java.nio.file.Path;
+import java.nio.file.StandardCopyOption;
+import java.nio.file.StandardOpenOption;
+import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
+import java.util.Map;
+import java.util.UUID;
+
+/** Assets are immutable; the atomic index replacement is the commit point. */
+public final class OledResourceStore {
+    public static final class CleanupFailure extends IOException {
+        public CleanupFailure(IOException cause) { super("本地索引已清除，但成品文件删除失败：" + cause.getMessage(), cause); }
+    }
+    private final Path root;
+    private final ObjectMapper mapper = new ObjectMapper();
+    private ObjectNode index;
+
+    public OledResourceStore() { this(Path.of(System.getProperty("user.home"), ".ahakey", "oled-resources", "v1")); }
+    public OledResourceStore(Path root) { this.root = root.toAbsolutePath().normalize(); }
+    public Path root() { return root; }
+
+    public synchronized Map<Integer, OledResourceState> load() throws IOException {
+        index = null;
+        if (Files.exists(root.resolve("index.json"))) {
+            if (Files.size(root.resolve("index.json")) > 1024 * 1024) throw new IOException("本地资源库索引过大。");
+            JsonNode parsed = mapper.readTree(root.resolve("index.json").toFile());
+            if (!(parsed instanceof ObjectNode object) || object.path("version").asInt(-1) != 1 || !object.path("entries").isObject())
+                throw new IOException("本地资源库索引格式无效，未覆盖原文件。");
+            index = object;
+        } else {
+            index = mapper.createObjectNode();
+            index.put("version", 1);
+            index.putObject("entries");
+        }
+        Map<Integer, OledResourceState> states = new LinkedHashMap<>();
+        try {
+            for (int entry = 0; entry < 19; entry++) {
+                JsonNode node = index.path("entries").path(Integer.toString(entry));
+                if (node.isMissingNode()) { states.put(entry, OledResourceState.empty(entry)); continue; }
+                Asset asset = null;
+                if (node.hasNonNull("file")) {
+                    Path path = assetPath(node.path("file").asText());
+                    int frames = node.path("frames").asInt(-1);
+                    if (frames < 1 || frames > 12 || Files.size(path) != frames * 28672L) throw new IOException("本地成品尺寸无效：目标 " + entry);
+                    asset = new Asset(Files.readAllBytes(path), frames, node.path("crc").asLong(-1));
+                }
+                Metadata device = node.hasNonNull("device") ? mapper.treeToValue(node.get("device"), Metadata.class) : null;
+                LocalStatus status = LocalStatus.valueOf(node.path("status").asText());
+                boolean interrupted = node.path("inFlight").asBoolean();
+                OledResourceState state = new OledResourceState(entry, asset, status, device, false, false,
+                        node.path("commitUncertain").asBoolean(),
+                        interrupted ? "上次上传被中断，设备保存结果未确认。" : node.path("notice").asText(""));
+                if (interrupted) state = state.failed("上次上传被中断，设备保存结果未确认。", node.path("commitUncertain").asBoolean());
+                states.put(entry, state);
+            }
+        } catch (IllegalArgumentException | IOException e) { index = null; throw new IOException("本地资源库内容无效：" + e.getMessage(), e); }
+        return states;
+    }
+
+    public synchronized boolean containsEntry(int entry) throws IOException {
+        requireLoaded();
+        return index.path("entries").has(Integer.toString(entry));
+    }
+
+    public synchronized void save(OledResourceState state) throws IOException {
+        requireLoaded();
+        Files.createDirectories(root);
+        ObjectNode next = index.deepCopy();
+        ObjectNode entries = (ObjectNode) next.get("entries");
+        JsonNode previous = entries.path(Integer.toString(state.entry()));
+        ObjectNode record = mapper.createObjectNode();
+        String file = null;
+        if (state.asset() != null) {
+            byte[] bytes = state.asset().bytes();
+            if (previous.hasNonNull("file") && previous.path("crc").asLong(-1) == state.asset().crc()
+                    && previous.path("frames").asInt(-1) == state.asset().frames()
+                    && Files.exists(assetPath(previous.path("file").asText()))
+                    && java.util.Arrays.equals(Files.readAllBytes(assetPath(previous.path("file").asText())), bytes)) {
+                file = previous.path("file").asText();
+            } else {
+                file = state.entry() + "-" + UUID.randomUUID() + ".rgb565";
+                atomicWrite(root.resolve(file), bytes);
+            }
+            record.put("file", file);
+            record.put("frames", state.asset().frames());
+            record.put("crc", state.asset().crc());
+        }
+        record.put("status", state.localStatus().name());
+        record.put("inFlight", state.inFlight());
+        record.put("commitUncertain", state.commitUncertain());
+        record.put("notice", state.notice());
+        record.set("device", mapper.valueToTree(state.device()));
+        entries.set(Integer.toString(state.entry()), record);
+        atomicWrite(root.resolve("index.json"), mapper.writerWithDefaultPrettyPrinter().writeValueAsBytes(next));
+        index = next;
+        // A cleared entry remains a tombstone so legacy migration cannot resurrect it.
+        if (file == null && previous.hasNonNull("file")) {
+            String old = previous.path("file").asText();
+            boolean shared = false;
+            for (JsonNode other : entries) if (old.equals(other.path("file").asText())) shared = true;
+            if (!shared) {
+                try { Files.deleteIfExists(assetPath(old)); }
+                catch (IOException e) { throw new CleanupFailure(e); }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    private void requireLoaded() throws IOException {
+        if (index == null) throw new IOException("本地资源库尚未成功读取，禁止覆盖。");
+    }
+    private Path assetPath(String name) throws IOException {
+        if (!name.matches("[0-9]{1,2}-[a-f0-9-]{36}\\.rgb565")) throw new IOException("本地成品路径无效。");
+        Path path = root.resolve(name).normalize();
+        if (!root.equals(path.getParent()) || Files.isSymbolicLink(path)) throw new IOException("本地成品路径越界。");
+        return path;
+    }
+    private void atomicWrite(Path target, byte[] bytes) throws IOException {
+        Path temporary = Files.createTempFile(root, ".pending-", ".tmp");
+        try {
+            try (FileChannel channel = FileChannel.open(temporary, StandardOpenOption.WRITE)) {
+                ByteBuffer buffer = ByteBuffer.wrap(bytes);
+                while (buffer.hasRemaining()) channel.write(buffer);
+                channel.force(true);
+            }
+            Files.move(temporary, target, StandardCopyOption.ATOMIC_MOVE, StandardCopyOption.REPLACE_EXISTING);
+        } finally { Files.deleteIfExists(temporary); }
+    }
+}

-    private OledUploadService() {
+    public static void uploadResource(BleManager ble, long generation, int entry,
+            com.example.ahakey.model.OledResourceState.Asset asset,
+            Consumer<Integer> acknowledgedPercent, BeforeCommit beforeCommit, Consumer<Progress> progress) throws Exception {
+        if (asset.frames() > AhaKeyProtocol.resourceCapacity(entry)) throw new IllegalArgumentException("frames");
+        byte[] bytes = asset.bytes();
+        boolean commitSent = false;
+        long transaction = IDS.incrementAndGet();
+        long start = System.nanoTime();
+        String phase = "BEGIN";
+        int acknowledged = 0;
+        logger.info("OLED upload start txn={} connection={} entry={} bytes={} transport={}", transaction, generation, entry, bytes.length,
+                ble.isUsbTransportOpen() ? "USB" : "BLE");
+        try (BleManager.DisplayLease lease = ble.openDisplayLease(true, generation)) {
+            try {
+                requireAck(ble.exchangeDisplay(lease, AhaKeyProtocol.resourceBegin(entry, asset.frames(), asset.crc())), AhaKeyProtocol.CMD_RESOURCE_BEGIN);
+                for (int offset = 0; offset < bytes.length; ) {
+                    phase = "CHUNK";
+                    int length = Math.min(AhaKeyProtocol.RESOURCE_CHUNK_BYTES, bytes.length - offset);
+                    requireAck(ble.exchangeDisplay(lease, AhaKeyProtocol.resourceChunk(entry, offset,
+                            java.util.Arrays.copyOfRange(bytes, offset, offset + length))), AhaKeyProtocol.CMD_RESOURCE_CHUNK);
+                    offset += length;
+                    acknowledged = offset;
+                    acknowledgedPercent.accept(offset * 100 / bytes.length);
+                    progress.accept(new Progress(offset, bytes.length, (System.nanoTime() - start) / 1_000_000));
+                }
+                phase = "LOCAL_COMMIT_RECORD";
+                beforeCommit.persist();
+                phase = "COMMIT";
+                commitSent = true;
+                requireAck(ble.exchangeDisplay(lease, AhaKeyProtocol.resourceCommit(entry)), AhaKeyProtocol.CMD_RESOURCE_COMMIT);
+            } catch (Exception e) {
+                // Quarantine before close can flush deferred writers while an upload may still be active.
+                if (!(e instanceof DeviceRejected)) ble.requireDisplayRecovery(generation);
+                throw e;
+            }
+        } catch (Exception e) {
+            logger.warn("OLED upload incomplete txn={} connection={} entry={} phase={} acknowledged={} elapsedMs={} failure={} recoveryRequired={}",
+                    transaction, generation, entry, phase, acknowledged, (System.nanoTime() - start) / 1_000_000,
+                    e.getClass().getSimpleName(), ble.isDisplayWriteUncertain());
+            throw new UploadFailure("上传并应用未完成（" + phase + "，已确认 " + acknowledged + "/" + bytes.length + " 字节）："
+                    + e.getMessage() + " 键盘资源有效性需重新同步确认。"
+                    + (ble.isDisplayWriteUncertain() ? " AI 状态发送已暂停；请断开并重新连接键盘后同步，本地资源已保留。" : ""), e,
+                    commitSent && !(e instanceof DeviceRejected));
+        }
+        logger.info("OLED upload committed txn={} connection={} entry={} bytes={} elapsedMs={}",
+                transaction, generation, entry, bytes.length, (System.nanoTime() - start) / 1_000_000);
     }

     public static EncodedFrame frameFromSingleImage(Path imagePath) throws IOException {
         validateGifSourceFileSize(imagePath);
-        BufferedImage source = ImageIO.read(imagePath.toFile());
-        if (source == null) {
-            throw new IOException("无法读取图片文件：" + imagePath);

+        try (ImageInputStream stream = ImageIO.createImageInputStream(imagePath.toFile())) {
+            if (stream == null) throw new IOException("文件不适用：无法打开图片。");
+            Iterator<ImageReader> readers = ImageIO.getImageReaders(stream);
+            if (!readers.hasNext()) throw new IOException("文件不适用：无法识别图片格式。");
+            ImageReader reader = readers.next();
+            try {
+                reader.setInput(stream, false);
+                String format = reader.getFormatName();
+                if (!format.equalsIgnoreCase("png") && !format.equalsIgnoreCase("jpeg") && !format.equalsIgnoreCase("jpg"))
+                    throw new IOException("文件不适用：仅支持 GIF、PNG 和 JPEG。");
+                validateDimensions(reader.getWidth(0), reader.getHeight(0));
+                return encodeFrame(reader.read(0));
+            } finally { reader.dispose(); }
+        }
+    }

+    public static List<EncodedFrame> importFile(Path path, int maxFrames) throws IOException {
+        String name = path.getFileName().toString().toLowerCase(java.util.Locale.ROOT);
+        return name.endsWith(".gif") ? framesFromGif(path, maxFrames) : List.of(frameFromSingleImage(path));
+    }
+
+    public static byte[] paddedStream(List<EncodedFrame> frames) {
+        if (frames.isEmpty() || frames.size() > 12) throw new IllegalArgumentException("frames");
+        byte[] bytes = new byte[frames.size() * AhaKeyProtocol.OLED_FRAME_SLOT_SIZE];
+        for (int i = 0; i < frames.size(); i++) {
+            if (frames.get(i).rgb565.length != AhaKeyProtocol.OLED_FRAME_BYTES) throw new IllegalArgumentException("RGB565 frame");
+            System.arraycopy(frames.get(i).rgb565, 0, bytes, i * AhaKeyProtocol.OLED_FRAME_SLOT_SIZE, AhaKeyProtocol.OLED_FRAME_BYTES);
+        }
+        return bytes;
+    }
+
+    public static BufferedImage decodeFrame(byte[] bytes, int offset) {
+        if (offset < 0 || bytes.length - offset < AhaKeyProtocol.OLED_FRAME_BYTES) throw new IllegalArgumentException("frame bytes");
+        BufferedImage image = new BufferedImage(160, 80, BufferedImage.TYPE_INT_RGB);
+        for (int y = 0; y < 80; y++) for (int x = 0; x < 160; x++) {
+            int value = ((bytes[offset++] & 255) << 8) | (bytes[offset++] & 255);
+            int r = (value >> 11) & 31, g = (value >> 5) & 63, b = value & 31;
+            image.setRGB(x, y, ((r * 255 / 31) << 16) | ((g * 255 / 63) << 8) | (b * 255 / 31));
+        }
+        return image;
+    }
+
+    private static void validateDimensions(int width, int height) throws IOException {
+        if (width <= 0 || height <= 0 || (long) width * height > MAX_DECODE_PIXELS)
+            throw new IOException("文件不适用：解码画布超过 16777216 像素上限。");
+    }
+
+    private static Node child(Node node, String name) throws IOException {
+        for (Node c = node.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNextSibling()) if (name.equals(c.getNodeName())) return c;
+        throw new IOException("文件不适用：GIF 缺少 " + name + "。");
+    }
+
+    private static int gifBackground(Node stream) throws IOException {
+        Node table = null;
+        for (Node node = stream.getFirstChild(); node != null; node = node.getNextSibling())
+            if ("GlobalColorTable".equals(node.getNodeName())) table = node;
+        if (table == null) return 0;
+        int index = attributeInt(table, "backgroundColorIndex");
+        for (Node color = table.getFirstChild(); color != null; color = color.getNextSibling()) {
+            if (attributeInt(color, "index") == index)
+                return 0xff000000 | (attributeInt(color, "red") << 16) | (attributeInt(color, "green") << 8) | attributeInt(color, "blue");
+        }
+        throw new IOException("文件不适用：GIF 背景色索引无效。");
+    }
+
+    private static String attribute(Node node, String name) throws IOException {
+        Node value = node.getAttributes().getNamedItem(name);
+        if (value == null) throw new IOException("文件不适用：GIF 缺少 " + name + "。");
+        return value.getNodeValue();
+    }
+
+    private static int attributeInt(Node node, String name) throws IOException {
+        try { return Integer.parseInt(attribute(node, name)); }
+        catch (NumberFormatException e) { throw new IOException("文件不适用：GIF 尺寸字段无效。", e); }
     }

+    private static Spinner<Integer> brightnessSpinner(com.example.ahakey.model.DisplayConfigState display) {
+        Spinner<Integer> spinner = new Spinner<>(1, 100, display.brightness.get());
+        spinner.setEditable(true);
+        spinner.setPrefWidth(96);
+        spinner.getValueFactory().setConverter(new javafx.util.StringConverter<>() {
+            @Override public String toString(Integer value) { return Integer.toString(value); }
+            @Override public Integer fromString(String text) {
+                if (text == null || text.isBlank()) return spinner.getValue();
+                try { return Math.max(1, Math.min(100, Integer.parseInt(text))); }
+                catch (NumberFormatException e) { return spinner.getValue(); }
+            }
+        });
+        spinner.getEditor().setTextFormatter(new javafx.scene.control.TextFormatter<String>(change ->
+                change.getControlNewText().matches("[0-9]{0,3}") ? change : null));
+        spinner.valueProperty().addListener((o, a, b) -> { if (b != null) display.editBrightness(b); });
+        return spinner;
+    }

+    private static Label displayReason(com.example.ahakey.model.DisplayConfigState display,
+            javafx.beans.property.ObjectProperty<AiRuntimeDisplayMode> current,
+            javafx.beans.property.BooleanProperty pending, AiRuntimeDisplayMode target) {
+        Label reason = new Label();

+        reason.getStyleClass().add("display-disabled-reason");
+        reason.textProperty().bind(Bindings.createStringBinding(() -> {
+            String key = display.disabledReasonKey.get();
+            if (key.isEmpty() && display.busy.get()) key = "display.operation-busy";
+            if (key.isEmpty() && display.loading.get()) key = "display.syncing";
+            if (key.isEmpty() && pending.get()) key = "display.mode-pending";
+            if (key.isEmpty() && target != null && current.get() != target)
+                key = target == AiRuntimeDisplayMode.SINGLE ? "display.single-disabled" : "display.multi-disabled";
+            return key.isEmpty() ? "" : LanguageManager.getInstance().getString(key);
+        }, display.disabledReasonKey, display.busy, display.loading, current, pending));
+        reason.visibleProperty().bind(reason.textProperty().isNotEmpty());
+        reason.managedProperty().bind(reason.visibleProperty());
+        return reason;
+    }
